SR² Hand documentation · last page change 2026-11-10

Revisions of the SR² Hand documentation pages

The 136 pages under docs/ have 1850 revisions in git. 11 pages have one, and the 15 most revised are listed below. Each revision links to its commit and to the page as that commit left it on GitHub. A result page is a Git LFS file, so GitHub shows it as a blob page with a “View raw” link. In a clone, git show COMMIT:PATH | git lfs smudge > /tmp/page.html writes an older version to open in a browser. History before the repository’s rewrite of 2026-10-09 keeps its old commit hashes in hand-archive.
